```python
    other = "/api/v1/syllabi/00000000-0000-4000-8000-0000000000ff/confirm"
    response = client.post(other, json=body(chosen(ITEM_ONE)), headers=auth)
    assert (response.status_code, response.json()["error"]["code"]) == (404, "NOT_FOUND")


def test_an_empty_list_is_refused(
    client: TestClient,
    auth: dict[str, str],
    syllabi: FakeSyllabusRepository,
    gcal: tuple[FakeCalendarRepository, FakeCalendarService],
) -> None:
    response = client.post(CONFIRM, json=body(), headers=auth)
    assert response.status_code == 422


def test_it_also_puts_the_chosen_items_in_the_calendar(
    client: TestClient,
    auth: dict[str, str],
    syllabi: FakeSyllabusRepository,
    gcal: tuple[FakeCalendarRepository, FakeCalendarService],
) -> None:
    calendar, service = gcal
    response = client.post(
        CONFIRM,
        json=body(chosen(ITEM_ONE), register_calendar=True),
        headers=auth,
    )
    assert response.status_code == 200
    assert response.json()["calendar_events_created"] == 1
    assert service.inserted[0][1] == "[시험] 데이터베이스 · 중간고사"
    # 시험은 **그 시각에 시작**해 1시간 뒤에 끝난다(공고·과제 마감과 반대다. 계획서 일정은 마감이
    # 아니라 시작 시각이라서, 캘린더 API로 넣을 때(`syllabus_item_id`)와 같은 시각이어야 한다)
    assert calendar.reserved[0]["starts_at"] == MIDTERM.astimezone(KST)
    assert service.inserted[0][2] == MIDTERM.astimezone(KST) + timedelta(hours=1)
    assert calendar.attached == [(calendar.reserved_id(), service.inserted[0][0])]
    assert calendar.commits == 1  # Google을 부르기 전에 자리를 확정한다


def test_no_calendar_connection_changes_nothing(
    client: TestClient,
    auth: dict[str, str],
    syllabi: FakeSyllabusRepository,
    gcal: tuple[FakeCalendarRepository, FakeCalendarService],
) -> None:
    """캘린더를 부탁했는데 연결이 없으면 확정도 하지 않는다(연결하고 다시 누르면 된다)."""
    calendar, service = gcal
    service.connected = False
    response = client.post(
        CONFIRM, json=body(chosen(ITEM_ONE), register_calendar=True), headers=auth
    )
    assert (response.status_code, response.json()["error"]["code"]) == (
        409,
        "CALENDAR_NOT_CONNECTED",
    )
    assert syllabi.confirmed == []
    assert calendar.reserved == []


def test_a_google_failure_still_keeps_the_confirmation(
    client: TestClient,
    auth: dict[str, str],
    syllabi: FakeSyllabusRepository,
    gcal: tuple[FakeCalendarRepository, FakeCalendarService],
) -> None:
    calendar, service = gcal
    service.fails = True
    response = client.post(
        CONFIRM, json=body(chosen(ITEM_ONE), register_calendar=True), headers=auth
    )
    assert response.status_code == 200
    assert response.json() == _counts(1, 1, 0)
    assert len(calendar.reserved) == 1  # 자리는 남는다 — 다시 누르면 같은 번호로 들어간다
    assert calendar.attached == []


def test_an_item_already_in_the_calendar_is_not_added_twice(
    client: TestClient,
    auth: dict[str, str],
    syllabi: FakeSyllabusRepository,
    gcal: tuple[FakeCalendarRepository, FakeCalendarService],
) -> None:
    calendar, service = gcal
    sent = body(chosen(ITEM_ONE), register_calendar=True)
    assert client.post(CONFIRM, json=sent, headers=auth).json()["calendar_events_created"] == 1
    again = client.post(CONFIRM, json=sent, headers=auth)
    assert again.json() == _counts(1, 0, 0)  # 할 일도 일정도 새로 만들지 않는다
    assert len(service.inserted) == 1


def test_a_past_item_is_confirmed_but_not_put_in_the_calendar(
    client: TestClient,
    auth: dict[str, str],
    syllabi: FakeSyllabusRepository,
    gcal: tuple[FakeCalendarRepository, FakeCalendarService],
) -> None:

    calendar, service = gcal
    syllabi.stored = [_stored(ITEM_ONE, "midterm", "중간고사", NOW - timedelta(days=3))]
    response = client.post(
        CONFIRM, json=body(chosen(ITEM_ONE), register_calendar=True), headers=auth
    )
    assert response.json() == _counts(1, 1, 0)
    assert calendar.reserved == []


def test_an_unselected_item_never_reaches_the_calendar(

    assert calendar.reserved == []

def test_the_same_item_twice_in_one_request_is_refused(

    """같은 일정을 두 번 보내면 센 수가 부풀고 확정과 캘린더가 어긋난다. 받지 않는다."""

        headers=auth,
    )
    assert (response.status_code, response.json()["error"]["code"]) == (422, "VALIDATION_FAILED")
    assert syllabi.confirmed == []

def test_a_blank_title_is_refused(
    client: TestClient,

) -> None:
    """공백만 적은 제목을 받으면 플래너에 제목 없는 줄이 생긴다(고칠 길도 없다)."""
    response = client.post(CONFIRM, json=body(chosen(ITEM_ONE, title="   ")), headers=auth)
    assert response.status_code == 422
    assert syllabi.confirmed == []


def test_a_fixed_time_moves_the_calendar_event_too(
    client: TestClient,
    auth: dict[str, str],
    syllabi: FakeSyllabusRepository,
    gcal: tuple[FakeCalendarRepository, FakeCalendarService],
) -> None:
    """시각을 고쳐 다시 확정하면 이미 넣어 둔 일정도 그 시각으로 옮긴다."""
    calendar, service = gcal
    first = client.post(CONFIRM, json=body(chosen(ITEM_ONE), register_calendar=True), headers=auth)
    assert first.json() == _counts(1, 1, 1)
    moved = client.post(
        CONFIRM,
        json=body(
            chosen(ITEM_ONE, title="중간고사(온라인)", starts_at="2026-11-04T13:00:00+09:00"),
            register_calendar=True,
        ),
        headers=auth,
    )
    assert moved.json() == _counts(1, 0, 0, moved=1)
    assert len(service.inserted) == 1  # 새로 넣지 않았다
    assert service.moved[0][1] == "[시험] 데이터베이스 · 중간고사(온라인)"
    assert service.moved[0][2].isoformat() == "2026-11-04T14:00:00+09:00"  # 끝 시각
    assert calendar.moved[0][1] == "[시험] 데이터베이스 · 중간고사(온라인)"
    assert calendar.moved[0][2].isoformat() == "2026-11-04T13:00:00+09:00"


def test_the_same_time_again_does_not_touch_google(
    client: TestClient,
    auth: dict[str, str],
    syllabi: FakeSyllabusRepository,

) -> None:
    calendar, service = gcal
    sent = body(chosen(ITEM_ONE), register_calendar=True)
    client.post(CONFIRM, json=sent, headers=auth)
    again = client.post(CONFIRM, json=sent, headers=auth)
    assert again.json() == _counts(1, 0, 0)
    assert (service.moved, calendar.moved) == ([], [])


def test_an_event_the_student_deleted_is_not_put_back(
    client: TestClient,
    auth: dict[str, str],
    syllabi: FakeSyllabusRepository,
    gcal: tuple[FakeCalendarRepository, FakeCalendarService],
) -> None:
    """학생이 구글 캘린더에서 지운 일정은 시각을 고쳐도 되살리지 않는다."""
    calendar, service = gcal
    client.post(CONFIRM, json=body(chosen(ITEM_ONE), register_calendar=True), headers=auth)
    service.gone = True
    again = client.post(
        CONFIRM,
        json=body(chosen(ITEM_ONE, starts_at="2026-11-04T13:00:00+09:00"), register_calendar=True),
        headers=auth,
    )
    assert again.json() == _counts(1, 0, 0)
    assert calendar.moved == []  # 우리 기록도 건드리지 않는다


def test_a_revoked_permission_asks_to_connect_again(
    client: TestClient,
    auth: dict[str, str],
    syllabi: FakeSyllabusRepository,
    gcal: tuple[FakeCalendarRepository, FakeCalendarService],
) -> None:
    """만료 전 토큰이 Google에서 거절당하면(권한 거둠) 409다 — 200에 "0건"으로 묻지 않는다."""
    calendar, service = gcal
    service.revoked = True
    response = client.post(
        CONFIRM, json=body(chosen(ITEM_ONE), register_calendar=True), headers=auth
    )
    assert (response.status_code, response.json()["error"]["code"]) == (
        409,
        "CALENDAR_NOT_CONNECTED",
    )
    assert response.json()["error"]["details"]["reason"] == "expired"


def test_one_failed_item_does_not_stop_the_others(
    client: TestClient,
    auth: dict[str, str],
    syllabi: FakeSyllabusRepository,
    gcal: tuple[FakeCalendarRepository, FakeCalendarService],
) -> None:
    """한 일정만 Google에서 막히면 그 건만 빠진다(센 수도 그만큼 적다)."""
    calendar, service = gcal

    inserts: list[str] = []

    async def flaky(token: str, *, event_id: str, title: str, ends_at: datetime) -> str:
        inserts.append(title)
        if len(inserts) == 1:
            raise ApiError(502, "GOOGLE_API_ERROR", "Google 캘린더가 응답하지 않아요.")
        return event_id

    service.insert = flaky  # type: ignore[method-assign]
    response = client.post(
        CONFIRM,
        json=body(
            chosen(ITEM_ONE),
            chosen(ITEM_TWO, title="팀 프로젝트 발표"),
            register_calendar=True,
        ),
        headers=auth,
    )
    assert response.json() == _counts(2, 2, 1)
    assert len(inserts) == 2  # 두 건 모두 시도했다


def test_a_syllabus_without_a_course_has_no_course_in_the_title(
    client: TestClient,
    auth: dict[str, str],
    syllabi: FakeSyllabusRepository,
    gcal: tuple[FakeCalendarRepository, FakeCalendarService],
) -> None:
    """LMS를 연결하지 않고 올린 계획서는 제목에 과목 자리가 없다."""
    calendar, service = gcal
    syllabi.heads = {(USER_ID, SYLLABUS_ID): SyllabusHead(SYLLABUS_ID, None, None, None)}
    client.post(CONFIRM, json=body(chosen(ITEM_ONE), register_calendar=True), headers=auth)
    assert service.inserted[0][1] == "[시험] 중간고사"
```

#### `api/tests/test_syllabi_db.py`

```python
"""계획서 추출 통합 테스트(F-14): 라우터 → 뒤에서 도는 실행 → 저장소·모델 → DB → `GET /runs/{id}`.

뒤에서 도는 일은 자기 연결(풀)을 열어 다른 연결이 쓴 행을 봐야 한다. 그래서 사용자·과목·파일을
커밋하고 끝나면 지운다. 일은 InlineRunner로 응답 전에 끝까지 돌린다. 모델은 가짜(ScriptedLlm)이고
Supabase Storage도 가짜다. 계획서 PDF는 reportlab으로 만든다(tests/samples.pdf).
"""

import json
from collections.abc import AsyncIterator, Callable
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import UTC, datetime
from typing import Any
from uuid import uuid4

import httpx
import pytest
import respx

from app.background import InlineRunner
from app.clock import current_time
from app.config import Settings, get_settings
from app.crypto import encrypt
from app.db import create_pools
from app.eligibility import KST
from app.llm.deps import get_llm
from app.main import create_app
from app.repositories.syllabi import SyllabusRepository
from app.schemas.syllabi import EXTRACT_TOOL
from app.uploads.storage import StorageError, get_storage
from tests import samples
from tests.conftest import make_token
from tests.db_world import DATABASE_URL, connect, make_user
from tests.llm_fakes import ScriptedLlm, call

pytestmark = [
    pytest.mark.db,
    pytest.mark.anyio,
    pytest.mark.skipif(not DATABASE_URL, reason="DATABASE_URL이 없어서 DB 통합 테스트를 건너뜀"),
]

SYLLABUS_TEXT = """2026학년도 2학기 강의계획서
교과목명: 데이터베이스 (202620HY23525)

주차별 계획
8주차 10/20(화) 중간고사 10:30~12:00
15주차 12/08 팀 프로젝트 발표
16주차 12/15 기말고사 10:30~12:00
"""
PDF = samples.pdf([SYLLABUS_TEXT])
ITEMS = [
    {
        "item_type": "midterm",
        "title": "중간고사",
        "date": "2026-10-20",
        "time": "10:30",
        "evidence_text": "8주차 10/20(화) 중간고사 10:30~12:00",
    },
    {
        "item_type": "presentation",
        "title": "팀 프로젝트 발표",
        "date": "2026-12-08",
        "evidence_text": "15주차 12/08 팀 프로젝트 발표",
    },
]


class FakeStorage:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.objects: dict[str, bytes] = {}
        self.asked: list[str] = []
        self.broken = False

    async def download(self, path: str) -> bytes:
        self.asked.append(path)
        if self.broken:
            raise StorageError("저장소가 거절했어요")
        if path not in self.objects:
            raise StorageError("그 파일이 없어요")
        return self.objects[path]

    async def upload(self, path: str, data: bytes, content_type: str) -> None:
        self.objects[path] = data

    async def remove(self, path: str) -> None:
        self.objects.pop(path, None)


@dataclass
class Fixture:
    user: str
    other: str
    courses: dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)
    files: dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)
    paths: dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)


@asynccontextmanager
async def committed() -> AsyncIterator[Fixture]:
    """사용자 둘, 과목 둘, 계획서 파일 둘을 커밋한다. 끝나면 지운다."""
    setup = await connect()
    user = await make_user(setup)
    other = await make_user(setup)
    world = Fixture(user, other)

    async def add_course(name: str, owner: str, canvas: int, *, active: bool = True) -> str:
        row = await (
            await setup.execute(
                "insert into lms_courses (user_id, canvas_course_id, name, is_active)"
                " values (%s, %s, %s, %s) returning id::text as id",
                (owner, canvas, name, active),
            )
        ).fetchone()
        assert row is not None
        world.courses[name] = row["id"]
        return row["id"]

    async def add_file(name: str, owner: str, *, kind: str = "syllabus") -> str:
        file_id = str(uuid4())
        path = f"{owner}/{kind}/{file_id}.pdf"
        await setup.execute(
            "insert into files (id, user_id, kind, origin, storage_path, mime_type, sha256)"
            " values (%s, %s, %s, 'upload', %s, 'application/pdf', %s)",
            (file_id, owner, kind, path, f"{abs(hash(name)):064d}"[:64]),
        )
        world.files[name] = file_id
        world.paths[file_id] = path
        return file_id

    await add_course("202620HY23525_데이터베이스", user, 9_900_101)
    await add_course("202620HY23534_기계학습", user, 9_900_102, active=False)
    await add_course("202620HY23599_남의과목", other, 9_900_103)
    await add_file("내 계획서", user)
    await add_file("남의 계획서", other)
    await add_file("내 포스터", user, kind="poster")
    await setup.commit()  # 뒤에서 도는 일은 자기 연결로 본다. 커밋해야 보인다
    try:
        yield world
    finally:
        await setup.execute("delete from auth.users where id = any(%s::uuid[])", ([user, other],))
        await setup.commit()
        await setup.close()


@asynccontextmanager
async def api(
    settings: Settings, llm: Any, storage: FakeStorage, *, now: datetime | None = None
) -> AsyncIterator[Callable[[str], httpx.AsyncClient]]:
    app = create_app(settings)
    if now is not None:  # 시계를 고정한다(지난 일정은 캘린더에 넣지 않으므로 날짜가 결과를 바꾼다)
        app.dependency_overrides[current_time] = lambda: now
    pool, log_pool = create_pools(DATABASE_URL)
    await pool.open()
    await log_pool.open()
    app.state.pool, app.state.log_pool, app.state.runner = pool, log_pool, InlineRunner()
    app.dependency_overrides[get_settings] = lambda: settings
    app.dependency_overrides[get_llm] = lambda: llm
    app.dependency_overrides[get_storage] = lambda: storage
    clients: list[httpx.AsyncClient] = []

    def client(user_id: str) -> httpx.AsyncClient:
        http = httpx.AsyncClient(
            transport=httpx.ASGITransport(app=app),
            base_url="http://test",
            headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {make_token(user_id)}"},
        )
        clients.append(http)
        return http

    try:
        yield client
    finally:
        for http in clients:
            await http.aclose()
        await pool.close()
        await log_pool.close()


async def extract(
    world: Fixture,
    settings: Settings,
    llm: Any,
    *,
    body: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
    storage: FakeStorage | None = None,
    name: str = "내 계획서",
    user: str | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """`POST /syllabi/extract` → `GET /runs/{id}`. InlineRunner라 응답 전에 실행이 끝나 있다."""
    store = storage or FakeStorage()
    file_id = world.files[name]
    store.objects.setdefault(world.paths[file_id], PDF)
    async with api(settings, llm, store) as client:
        http = client(user or world.user)
        started = await http.post(
            "/api/v1/syllabi/extract", json={"file_id": file_id, **(body or {})}
        )
        assert started.status_code == 202, started.text
        run_id = started.json()["run_id"]
        return (await http.get(f"/api/v1/runs/{run_id}")).json()


async def test_a_syllabus_becomes_items_the_confirm_screen_can_show(settings: Settings) -> None:
    llm = ScriptedLlm(
        call(EXTRACT_TOOL, items=ITEMS, course_name="데이터베이스", semester="2026-2")
    )
    async with committed() as world:
        course = world.courses["202620HY23525_데이터베이스"]
        run = await extract(world, settings, llm, body={"lms_course_id": course})

    assert run["status"] == "succeeded", run
    result = run["result"]
    assert result["kind"] == "syllabus_extract"
    assert result["course_name"] == "데이터베이스"
    assert [(one["item_type"], one["title"]) for one in result["items"]] == [
        ("midterm", "중간고사"),
        ("presentation", "팀 프로젝트 발표"),
    ]
    assert result["items"][0]["starts_at"] == "2026-10-20T10:30:00+09:00"  # KST로 나간다
    assert result["items"][0]["is_confirmed"] is False  # 확인 화면에서 고른 것만 등록한다
    # 실행 기록: PDF 읽기 → 저장(명세 11장의 처리 과정). 화면에는 한국어 이름으로 보인다
    assert [step["tool"] for step in run["steps"]] == ["read_syllabus_pdf", "save_syllabus"]
    assert [step["label"] for step in run["steps"]] == ["계획서 글자 읽기", "시험·발표 일정 저장"]


async def test_the_model_sees_the_text_and_the_semester_of_that_course(
    settings: Settings,
) -> None:
    """연도 없는 날짜를 읽는 기준은 **과목명에서 센 학기 시작일**이다(2026-2 → 9/1)."""
    llm = ScriptedLlm(call(EXTRACT_TOOL, items=[]))
    async with committed() as world:
        course = world.courses["202620HY23525_데이터베이스"]
        await extract(world, settings, llm, body={"lms_course_id": course})

    sent = str(llm.requests[0].turns[0].text)
    assert "학기 범위(KST): 2026-09-01 ~ 2027-01-29" in sent
    assert "8주차 10/20(화) 중간고사" in sent  # PDF에서 뽑은 글자가 그대로 간다


async def test_items_are_saved_unconfirmed_and_only_once_per_course(settings: Settings) -> None:
    llm = ScriptedLlm(
        call(EXTRACT_TOOL, items=ITEMS, course_name="데이터베이스"),
        call(EXTRACT_TOOL, items=ITEMS[:1], course_name="데이터베이스"),
    )
    async with committed() as world:
        course = world.courses["202620HY23525_데이터베이스"]
        first = await extract(world, settings, llm, body={"lms_course_id": course})
        again = await extract(world, settings, llm, body={"lms_course_id": course, "replace": True})
        conn = await connect()
        try:
            rows = await (
                await conn.execute(
                    "select s.id::text as id, s.course_name, s.semester, count(i.id) as items"
                    " from syllabi s left join syllabus_items i on i.syllabus_id = s.id"
                    " where s.user_id = %s group by s.id, s.course_name, s.semester",
                    (world.user,),
                )
            ).fetchall()
            confirmed = await (
                await conn.execute(
                    "select bool_or(is_confirmed) as any_confirmed from syllabus_items i"
                    " join syllabi s on s.id = i.syllabus_id where s.user_id = %s",
                    (world.user,),
                )
            ).fetchone()
        finally:
            await conn.close()

    assert first["result"]["syllabus_id"] != again["result"]["syllabus_id"]
    assert len(rows) == 1  # 과목당 하나다. 다시 올리면 갈아 끼운다
    assert rows[0]["items"] == 1  # 옛 일정은 함께 지워졌다
    assert confirmed is not None and confirmed["any_confirmed"] is False


async def test_a_second_syllabus_for_the_same_course_needs_replace(settings: Settings) -> None:
    llm = ScriptedLlm(call(EXTRACT_TOOL, items=ITEMS))
    async with committed() as world:
        course = world.courses["202620HY23525_데이터베이스"]
        await extract(world, settings, llm, body={"lms_course_id": course})
        store = FakeStorage()
        file_id = world.files["내 계획서"]
        store.objects[world.paths[file_id]] = PDF
        async with api(settings, llm, store) as client:
            response = await client(world.user).post(
                "/api/v1/syllabi/extract", json={"file_id": file_id, "lms_course_id": course}
            )
    assert (response.status_code, response.json()["error"]["code"]) == (409, "SYLLABUS_EXISTS")


async def test_a_syllabus_without_a_course_is_saved_too(settings: Settings) -> None:
    """LMS를 연결하지 않은 학생도 올릴 수 있다. 과목 연결 없이 저장한다."""
    llm = ScriptedLlm(call(EXTRACT_TOOL, items=ITEMS[:1], course_name="데이터베이스"))
    async with committed() as world:
        run = await extract(world, settings, llm)
        conn = await connect()
        try:
            row = await (
                await conn.execute(
                    "select lms_course_id, course_name from syllabi where user_id = %s",
                    (world.user,),
                )
            ).fetchone()
        finally:
            await conn.close()
    assert run["status"] == "succeeded"
    assert row is not None and row["lms_course_id"] is None
    # 과목이 없으면 학기 범위는 올린 날 기준이다(그래도 10/20은 그 범위 안이다)
    assert run["result"]["items"][0]["title"] == "중간고사"


async def test_someone_elses_course_is_not_found(settings: Settings) -> None:
    llm = ScriptedLlm(call(EXTRACT_TOOL, items=[]))
    async with committed() as world:
        store = FakeStorage()
        file_id = world.files["내 계획서"]
        store.objects[world.paths[file_id]] = PDF
        async with api(settings, llm, store) as client:
            theirs = await client(world.user).post(
                "/api/v1/syllabi/extract",
                json={"file_id": file_id, "lms_course_id": world.courses["202620HY23599_남의과목"]},
            )
            dropped = await client(world.user).post(
                "/api/v1/syllabi/extract",
                json={"file_id": file_id, "lms_course_id": world.courses["202620HY23534_기계학습"]},
            )
    assert theirs.status_code == 404  # 남의 과목
    assert dropped.status_code == 404  # 수강을 뺀 과목


async def test_another_students_file_is_not_found(settings: Settings) -> None:

            )

async def test_a_poster_is_not_a_syllabus(settings: Settings) -> None:

async def test_a_scanned_pdf_fails_with_a_clear_reason(settings: Settings) -> None:
    """글자가 없는 PDF(사진을 감싼 것)는 모델을 부르지 않고 끝낸다."""
    llm = ScriptedLlm(call(EXTRACT_TOOL, items=[]))
    async with committed() as world:
        store = FakeStorage()
        store.objects[world.paths[world.files["내 계획서"]]] = samples.pdf([None])
        run = await extract(world, settings, llm, storage=store)
    assert run["status"] == "failed"

async def test_a_broken_storage_fails_the_run(settings: Settings) -> None:

async def test_without_a_model_nothing_is_saved(settings: Settings) -> None:

        run = await extract(world, settings, None)
        conn = await connect()
        try:

                await conn.execute(
                    "select count(*) as count from syllabi where user_id = %s", (world.user,)
                )
            ).fetchone()
        finally:
            await conn.close()
    assert run["status"] == "failed" and run["error"]["code"] == "LLM_ERROR"
    assert rows is not None and rows["count"] == 0


async def test_the_repository_reads_only_my_rows(settings: Settings) -> None:
    async with committed() as world:
        conn = await connect()
        try:
            repo = SyllabusRepository(conn)
            assert await repo.syllabus_file(world.user, world.files["내 계획서"]) is not None
            assert await repo.syllabus_file(world.user, world.files["남의 계획서"]) is None
            assert await repo.syllabus_file(world.other, world.files["내 계획서"]) is None
            mine = await repo.course(world.user, world.courses["202620HY23525_데이터베이스"])
            assert mine is not None
            assert (mine.short_name, mine.semester_start.isoformat()) == (
                "데이터베이스",
                "2026-09-01",
            )
            assert await repo.course(world.other, mine.id) is None
        finally:
            await conn.close()


async def test_the_model_cannot_invent_an_exam_date(settings: Settings) -> None:
    """근거 줄이 계획서에 없는 일정은 저장하지 않는다(DB까지 확인한다)."""
    llm = ScriptedLlm(
        call(
            EXTRACT_TOOL,
            items=[
                {
                    "item_type": "final",
                    "title": "기말고사",
                    "date": "2026-12-22",
                    "evidence_text": "계획서에 없는 줄",
                },
                ITEMS[0],
            ],
        )
    )
    async with committed() as world:
        run = await extract(world, settings, llm)
        conn = await connect()
        try:
            rows = await (
                await conn.execute(
                    "select i.title, i.starts_at from syllabus_items i"
                    " join syllabi s on s.id = i.syllabus_id where s.user_id = %s",
                    (world.user,),
                )
            ).fetchall()
        finally:
            await conn.close()
    assert [row["title"] for row in rows] == ["중간고사"]
    assert rows[0]["starts_at"].astimezone(KST).isoformat() == "2026-10-20T10:30:00+09:00"
    assert run["result"]["items"] == [
        {
            "id": run["result"]["items"][0]["id"],
            "item_type": "midterm",
            "title": "중간고사",
            "starts_at": "2026-10-20T10:30:00+09:00",
            "is_confirmed": False,
        }
    ]


async def test_nothing_found_keeps_the_old_syllabus(settings: Settings) -> None:
    """일정을 하나도 못 찾았으면 **먼저 올린 계획서를 그대로 둔다**.

    덮어쓰면 그 일정으로 만든 할 일·캘린더 기록까지 cascade로 사라진다.
    """
    llm = ScriptedLlm(
        call(EXTRACT_TOOL, items=ITEMS, course_name="데이터베이스"),
        call(EXTRACT_TOOL, items=[]),  # 두 번째 PDF에서는 아무것도 못 찾았다
    )
    async with committed() as world:
        course = world.courses["202620HY23525_데이터베이스"]
        first = await extract(world, settings, llm, body={"lms_course_id": course})
        again = await extract(world, settings, llm, body={"lms_course_id": course, "replace": True})
        conn = await connect()
        try:
            rows = await (
                await conn.execute(
                    "select s.id::text as id, count(i.id) as items from syllabi s"
                    " left join syllabus_items i on i.syllabus_id = s.id"
                    " where s.user_id = %s group by s.id",
                    (world.user,),
                )
            ).fetchall()
        finally:
            await conn.close()

    assert again["status"] == "failed"
    assert again["error"]["code"] == "SYLLABUS_READ_FAILED"
    assert "그대로 두었으니" in again["error"]["message"]
    assert [(row["id"], row["items"]) for row in rows] == [
        (first["result"]["syllabus_id"], len(ITEMS))
    ]


async def test_a_syllabus_with_no_items_is_still_saved_the_first_time(
    settings: Settings,
) -> None:
    """시험 일정이 안 적힌 계획서도 있다. 처음 올리는 것은 그대로 저장한다(일정 0개)."""
    llm = ScriptedLlm(call(EXTRACT_TOOL, items=[], course_name="데이터베이스"))
    async with committed() as world:
        course = world.courses["202620HY23525_데이터베이스"]
        run = await extract(world, settings, llm, body={"lms_course_id": course})
    assert run["status"] == "succeeded"
    assert run["result"]["items"] == []


async def test_a_model_without_the_tool_fails_the_run(settings: Settings) -> None:
    """모델이 도구를 안 부르면 학생에게 다시 올리라고 한다."""
    llm = ScriptedLlm("일정을 찾지 못했습니다")
    async with committed() as world:
        run = await extract(world, settings, llm)
    assert run["status"] == "failed"
    assert run["error"]["code"] == "SYLLABUS_READ_FAILED"
    assert "PDF로 저장해 올려 주세요" in run["error"]["message"]


async def test_a_model_that_breaks_is_an_llm_error(settings: Settings) -> None:
    class Broken:
        provider = "google"

        async def generate(self, **_: Any) -> Any:
            raise RuntimeError("모델이 터졌다")

    async with committed() as world:
        run = await extract(world, settings, Broken())
    assert run["status"] == "failed" and run["error"]["code"] == "LLM_ERROR"


async def test_a_model_that_is_too_slow_is_a_timeout(settings: Settings) -> None:
    class Slow:
        provider = "google"

        async def generate(self, **_: Any) -> Any:
            raise TimeoutError("늦었다")

    async with committed() as world:
        run = await extract(world, settings, Slow())
    assert run["status"] == "failed" and run["error"]["code"] == "LLM_TIMEOUT"


async def test_a_broken_pdf_in_the_storage_fails_with_a_clear_reason(
    settings: Settings,
) -> None:
    """업로드는 지나갔지만 저장소의 파일이 깨졌을 때(글자를 못 뽑는다)."""
    llm = ScriptedLlm(call(EXTRACT_TOOL, items=[]))
    async with committed() as world:
        store = FakeStorage()
        store.objects[world.paths[world.files["내 계획서"]]] = b"%PDF-1.4 broken"
        run = await extract(world, settings, llm, storage=store)
    assert run["status"] == "failed" and run["error"]["code"] == "SYLLABUS_READ_FAILED"
    assert llm.requests == []


# ── 확정 (F-14 2부, `POST /syllabi/{id}/confirm`) ───────────────────────────

NOW = datetime(2026, 10, 17, 1, 0, tzinfo=UTC)  # 10/17 10:00 KST. 10/20 시험이 아직 남아 있다
MODEL_COURSE_NAME = "데이터베이스시스템"  # 모델이 계획서에서 읽은 과목 이름(LMS 과목명과 다르다)
MIDTERM_DATE = "2026-10-20"


@asynccontextmanager
async def confirmed(
    settings: Settings,
    world: Fixture,
    *,
    items: list[dict[str, Any]] = ITEMS,
    name: str = "내 계획서",
) -> AsyncIterator[tuple[Callable[[str], httpx.AsyncClient], str, list[dict[str, Any]]]]:
    """추출까지 끝낸 상태로 넘긴다: (클라이언트 공장, 계획서 id, 뽑힌 일정들)."""
    # 모델은 과목 이름을 계획서 표지에서 읽는다 — LMS 과목명과 다를 수 있다. 제목·칩은 **LMS 이름**이
    # 이겨야 한다(어느 길로 넣어도 같은 제목이 되게)
    llm = ScriptedLlm(call(EXTRACT_TOOL, items=items, course_name=MODEL_COURSE_NAME))
    store = FakeStorage()
    file_id = world.files[name]
    store.objects[world.paths[file_id]] = PDF
    course = world.courses["202620HY23525_데이터베이스"]
    async with api(settings, llm, store, now=NOW) as client:
        http = client(world.user)
        started = await http.post(
            "/api/v1/syllabi/extract", json={"file_id": file_id, "lms_course_id": course}
        )
        assert started.status_code == 202, started.text
        run = (await http.get(f"/api/v1/runs/{started.json()['run_id']}")).json()
        assert run["status"] == "succeeded", run
        yield client, run["result"]["syllabus_id"], run["result"]["items"]


def _counts(confirmed: int, tasks: int, events: int, moved: int = 0) -> dict[str, int]:
    return {
        "confirmed": confirmed,
        "planner_tasks_created": tasks,
        "calendar_events_created": events,
        "calendar_events_moved": moved,
    }


def _chosen(items: list[dict[str, Any]], *titles: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """뽑힌 일정 중 제목이 맞는 것만 고른다(나머지는 선택을 뺀 채로 보낸다)."""
    return [
        {"id": one["id"], "title": one["title"], "selected": one["title"] in titles}
        for one in items
    ]


async def test_confirming_saves_the_items_and_makes_planner_tasks(settings: Settings) -> None:
    async with committed() as world:
        async with confirmed(settings, world) as (client, syllabus_id, items):
            http = client(world.user)
            response = await http.post(
                f"/api/v1/syllabi/{syllabus_id}/confirm", json={"items": _chosen(items, "중간고사")}
            )
            assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
            assert response.json() == _counts(1, 1, 0)
            planner = await http.get(
                "/api/v1/planner/tasks", params={"from": "2026-10-01", "to": "2026-12-31"}
            )
            assert planner.status_code == 200, planner.text
            tasks = [one for one in planner.json()["items"] if one["source"] == "syllabus"]

    assert len(tasks) == 1  # 고르지 않은 발표는 할 일이 되지 않는다
    task = tasks[0]
    assert (task["title"], task["due_date"], task["is_done"]) == ("중간고사", MIDTERM_DATE, False)
    # 명세 7장: 계획서에서 온 할 일은 `link.type = syllabus_item`이고 칩에 과목 이름이 보인다
    assert task["link"]["type"] == "syllabus_item"
    assert task["link"]["title"] == "데이터베이스"  # LMS 과목명이 이긴다(모델이 읽은 이름이 아니다)
    assert task["link"]["id"] == next(one["id"] for one in items if one["title"] == "중간고사")


async def test_confirming_twice_keeps_one_task(settings: Settings) -> None:
    async with committed() as world:
        async with confirmed(settings, world) as (client, syllabus_id, items):
            http = client(world.user)
            path = f"/api/v1/syllabi/{syllabus_id}/confirm"
            sent = {"items": _chosen(items, "중간고사")}
            first = await http.post(path, json=sent)
            again = await http.post(path, json=sent)
            planner = await http.get(
                "/api/v1/planner/tasks", params={"from": "2026-10-01", "to": "2026-12-31"}
            )
            tasks = [one for one in planner.json()["items"] if one["source"] == "syllabus"]

    assert first.json()["planner_tasks_created"] == 1
    assert again.json()["planner_tasks_created"] == 0  # 이미 있는 할 일은 맞추기만 한다
    assert len(tasks) == 1


async def test_a_fixed_title_and_time_move_the_task(settings: Settings) -> None:
    """확인 화면에서 날짜를 고치면 일정과 할 일이 함께 움직인다."""
    async with committed() as world:
        async with confirmed(settings, world) as (client, syllabus_id, items):
            http = client(world.user)
            midterm = next(one for one in items if one["title"] == "중간고사")
            path = f"/api/v1/syllabi/{syllabus_id}/confirm"
            await http.post(path, json={"items": _chosen(items, "중간고사")})
            moved = await http.post(
                path,
                json={
                    "items": [
                        {
                            "id": midterm["id"],
                            "title": "중간고사(온라인)",
                            "starts_at": "2026-10-22T13:00:00+09:00",
                            "selected": True,
                        }
                    ]
                },
            )
            assert moved.status_code == 200, moved.text
            planner = await http.get(
                "/api/v1/planner/tasks", params={"from": "2026-10-01", "to": "2026-12-31"}
            )
            tasks = [one for one in planner.json()["items"] if one["source"] == "syllabus"]

    assert moved.json()["planner_tasks_created"] == 0
    assert (tasks[0]["title"], tasks[0]["due_date"]) == ("중간고사(온라인)", "2026-10-22")


async def test_unselecting_removes_the_task_but_keeps_a_finished_one(settings: Settings) -> None:
    async with committed() as world:
        async with confirmed(settings, world) as (client, syllabus_id, items):
            http = client(world.user)
            path = f"/api/v1/syllabi/{syllabus_id}/confirm"
            await http.post(path, json={"items": _chosen(items, "중간고사", "팀 프로젝트 발표")})
            planner = await http.get(
                "/api/v1/planner/tasks", params={"from": "2026-10-01", "to": "2026-12-31"}
            )
            tasks = {
                one["title"]: one["id"]
                for one in planner.json()["items"]
                if one["source"] == "syllabus"
            }
            assert set(tasks) == {"중간고사", "팀 프로젝트 발표"}
            # 발표 할 일은 끝냈다. 선택을 풀어도 체크한 것을 되돌리지 않는다
            done = await http.patch(
                f"/api/v1/planner/tasks/{tasks['팀 프로젝트 발표']}", json={"is_done": True}
            )
            assert done.status_code == 200, done.text
            dropped = await http.post(path, json={"items": _chosen(items)})
            after = await http.get(
                "/api/v1/planner/tasks", params={"from": "2026-10-01", "to": "2026-12-31"}
            )
            left = [one for one in after.json()["items"] if one["source"] == "syllabus"]

    assert dropped.json() == _counts(0, 0, 0)
    assert [(one["title"], one["is_done"]) for one in left] == [("팀 프로젝트 발표", True)]


EARLY_ITEM = [
    {
        "item_type": "presentation",
        "title": "팀 프로젝트 발표",
        "date": "2026-12-08",
        "time": "08:00",  # 1교시 발표. UTC로는 **전날** 23:00이다
        "evidence_text": "15주차 12/08 팀 프로젝트 발표",
    }
]


async def test_an_early_morning_item_keeps_its_kst_day(settings: Settings) -> None:
    """1교시(08:00) 일정은 UTC로 전날이다. 저장해 둔 시각으로 만드는 할 일 날짜도 **KST 날짜**다."""
    async with committed() as world:
        async with confirmed(settings, world, items=EARLY_ITEM) as (client, syllabus_id, items):
            http = client(world.user)
            # 추출이 KST 08:00으로 저장했는지 먼저 본다(여기가 어긋나면 아래 날짜도 뜻이 없다)
            assert items[0]["starts_at"] == "2026-12-08T08:00:00+09:00"
            # 시각을 고치지 않고 그대로 확정한다 → 날짜는 DB에 UTC로 든 값에서 센다
            response = await http.post(
                f"/api/v1/syllabi/{syllabus_id}/confirm",
                json={
                    "items": [{"id": items[0]["id"], "title": "팀 프로젝트 발표", "selected": True}]
                },
            )
            assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
            planner = await http.get(
                "/api/v1/planner/tasks", params={"from": "2026-12-01", "to": "2026-12-31"}
            )
            tasks = [one for one in planner.json()["items"] if one["source"] == "syllabus"]

    assert [one["due_date"] for one in tasks] == ["2026-12-08"]  # 12/07이 아니다


async def test_another_students_syllabus_cannot_be_confirmed(settings: Settings) -> None:
    async with committed() as world:
        async with confirmed(settings, world) as (client, syllabus_id, items):
            other = client(world.other)
            response = await other.post(
                f"/api/v1/syllabi/{syllabus_id}/confirm", json={"items": _chosen(items, "중간고사")}
            )
    assert (response.status_code, response.json()["error"]["code"]) == (404, "NOT_FOUND")


async def test_an_item_from_another_syllabus_is_refused(settings: Settings) -> None:
    """남의 계획서 일정 id를 내 계획서에 섞어 보내도 아무것도 바뀌지 않는다."""
    async with committed() as world:
        async with confirmed(settings, world) as (client, syllabus_id, items):
            http = client(world.user)
            unknown = {"id": str(uuid4()), "title": "중간고사", "selected": True}
            response = await http.post(
                f"/api/v1/syllabi/{syllabus_id}/confirm", json={"items": [unknown]}
            )
            planner = await http.get(
                "/api/v1/planner/tasks", params={"from": "2026-10-01", "to": "2026-12-31"}
            )
            tasks = [one for one in planner.json()["items"] if one["source"] == "syllabus"]

    assert (response.status_code, response.json()["error"]["code"]) == (422, "VALIDATION_FAILED")
    assert tasks == []


GOOGLE_BASE = "https://calendar.test/v3"
GOOGLE_INSERT = f"{GOOGLE_BASE}/calendars/primary/events"


def _with_google(settings: Settings) -> Settings:
    return settings.model_copy(
        update={
            "google_calendar_api_base": GOOGLE_BASE,
            "google_token_url": "https://oauth.test/token",
            "google_oauth_client_id": "client-1",
            "google_oauth_client_secret": "secret-1",
        }
    )


async def _connect_google(user: str, key: str) -> None:
    """Google 캘린더 연결을 넣는다. 토큰은 암호화해 저장한다(평문으로 두지 않는다)."""
    setup = await connect()
    try:
        await setup.execute(
            "insert into oauth_tokens (user_id, provider, access_token, refresh_token, expires_at,"
            " scope) values (%s, 'google', %s, %s, now() + interval '30 minutes', 'calendar')"
            " on conflict (user_id, provider) do update set access_token = excluded.access_token",
            (user, encrypt("access-1", key), encrypt("refresh-1", key)),
        )
        await setup.commit()
    finally:
        await setup.close()


async def test_confirming_with_the_calendar_writes_one_event(settings: Settings) -> None:
    """고른 일정이 플래너와 Google 캘린더에 각각 하나씩 들어간다(F-14 → F-32 기록)."""
    tuned = _with_google(settings)
    async with committed() as world:
        await _connect_google(world.user, tuned.token_encryption_key)
        async with confirmed(tuned, world) as (client, syllabus_id, items):
            http = client(world.user)
            with respx.mock(assert_all_called=True) as router:
                route = router.post(GOOGLE_INSERT).mock(
                    return_value=httpx.Response(200, json={"id": "gcal-midterm"})
                )
                response = await http.post(
                    f"/api/v1/syllabi/{syllabus_id}/confirm",
                    json={"items": _chosen(items, "중간고사"), "register_calendar": True},
                )
            assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
            sent = json.loads(route.calls[0].request.content)
            rows = await _events(world.user)

    assert response.json() == _counts(1, 1, 1)
    assert sent["summary"] == "[시험] 데이터베이스 · 중간고사"
    assert sent["start"]["dateTime"].startswith("2026-10-20T10:30")
    assert sent["end"]["dateTime"].startswith("2026-10-20T11:30")
    assert len(rows) == 1
    assert rows[0]["title"] == "[시험] 데이터베이스 · 중간고사"
    # 일정 번호는 **우리 행 id**에서 만들어 보낸 것이다(다시 눌러도 캘린더에 하나만 들어간다)
    assert rows[0]["external_event_id"] == sent["id"]
    assert rows[0]["syllabus_item_id"] is not None


async def test_confirming_twice_with_the_calendar_keeps_one_event(settings: Settings) -> None:
    tuned = _with_google(settings)
    async with committed() as world:
        await _connect_google(world.user, tuned.token_encryption_key)
        async with confirmed(tuned, world) as (client, syllabus_id, items):
            http = client(world.user)
            sent = {"items": _chosen(items, "중간고사"), "register_calendar": True}
            path = f"/api/v1/syllabi/{syllabus_id}/confirm"
            with respx.mock(assert_all_called=True) as router:
                first_route = router.post(GOOGLE_INSERT).mock(
                    return_value=httpx.Response(200, json={"id": "gcal-midterm"})
                )
                first = await http.post(path, json=sent)
                asked = json.loads(first_route.calls[0].request.content)["id"]
            with respx.mock(assert_all_called=False) as router:
                route = router.post(GOOGLE_INSERT).mock(
                    return_value=httpx.Response(200, json={"id": "gcal-again"})
                )
                again = await http.post(path, json=sent)
            rows = await _events(world.user)

    assert first.json()["calendar_events_created"] == 1
    assert again.json()["calendar_events_created"] == 0
    assert not route.called  # 이미 넣은 일정은 Google을 다시 부르지 않는다
    assert len(rows) == 1 and rows[0]["external_event_id"] == asked


async def test_without_a_connection_nothing_is_confirmed(settings: Settings) -> None:
    """캘린더를 부탁했는데 연결이 없으면 확정도 하지 않는다(연결한 뒤 다시 누르면 된다)."""
    tuned = _with_google(settings)
    async with committed() as world:
        async with confirmed(tuned, world) as (client, syllabus_id, items):
            http = client(world.user)
            response = await http.post(
                f"/api/v1/syllabi/{syllabus_id}/confirm",
                json={"items": _chosen(items, "중간고사"), "register_calendar": True},
            )
            planner = await http.get(
                "/api/v1/planner/tasks", params={"from": "2026-10-01", "to": "2026-12-31"}
            )
            tasks = [one for one in planner.json()["items"] if one["source"] == "syllabus"]
            rows = await _events(world.user)

    assert response.status_code == 409
    assert response.json()["error"]["code"] == "CALENDAR_NOT_CONNECTED"
    assert (tasks, rows) == ([], [])


async def test_a_confirmed_item_can_also_go_in_through_the_calendar_api(
    settings: Settings,
) -> None:
    """확인한 일정은 `POST /calendar/events`의 `syllabus_item_id`로도 넣을 수 있다(명세 7장)."""
    tuned = _with_google(settings)
    async with committed() as world:
        await _connect_google(world.user, tuned.token_encryption_key)
        async with confirmed(tuned, world) as (client, syllabus_id, items):
            http = client(world.user)
            item_id = next(one["id"] for one in items if one["title"] == "중간고사")
            before = await http.post("/api/v1/calendar/events", json={"syllabus_item_id": item_id})
            await http.post(
                f"/api/v1/syllabi/{syllabus_id}/confirm", json={"items": _chosen(items, "중간고사")}
            )
            with respx.mock(assert_all_called=True) as router:
                route = router.post(GOOGLE_INSERT).mock(
                    return_value=httpx.Response(200, json={"id": "gcal-direct"})
                )
                after = await http.post(
                    "/api/v1/calendar/events", json={"syllabus_item_id": item_id}
                )
                asked = json.loads(route.calls[0].request.content)["id"]

    # 확인 전에는 넣을 수 없다(추출한 날짜가 틀릴 수 있다)
    assert (before.status_code, before.json()["error"]["code"]) == (404, "NOT_FOUND")
    assert after.status_code == 201, after.text
    body = after.json()
    assert body["title"] == "[시험] 데이터베이스 · 중간고사"
    assert body["starts_at"] == "2026-10-20T10:30:00+09:00"
    assert body["external_event_id"] == asked


async def _events(user: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """그 사용자의 캘린더 기록(다른 연결로 커밋된 것을 본다)."""
    setup = await connect()
    try:
        rows = await (
            await setup.execute(
                "select title, external_event_id, starts_at,"
                " syllabus_item_id::text as syllabus_item_id from calendar_events"
                " where user_id = %s order by starts_at",
                (user,),
            )
        ).fetchall()
        return [dict(row) for row in rows]
    finally:
        await setup.close()


async def test_a_permission_taken_away_mid_flight_ends_with_409(settings: Settings) -> None:
    """만료 전 토큰이 Google에서 401로 거절되면(권한 거둠) 409로 끝난다.

    그때 확정은 **이미 저장돼 있다** — 캘린더 자리를 Google 호출 전에 커밋하기 때문이다(그래야
    다시 눌러도 일정이 둘로 늘지 않는다). 학생이 다시 연결해 누르면 그 자리로 이어 넣는다.
    """
    tuned = _with_google(settings)
    async with committed() as world:
        await _connect_google(world.user, tuned.token_encryption_key)
        async with confirmed(tuned, world) as (client, syllabus_id, items):
            http = client(world.user)
            sent = {"items": _chosen(items, "중간고사"), "register_calendar": True}
            path = f"/api/v1/syllabi/{syllabus_id}/confirm"
            with respx.mock(assert_all_called=True) as router:
                router.post(GOOGLE_INSERT).mock(return_value=httpx.Response(401, json={}))
                refused = await http.post(path, json=sent)
            rows = await _events(world.user)
            planner = await http.get(
                "/api/v1/planner/tasks", params={"from": "2026-10-01", "to": "2026-12-31"}
            )
            tasks = [one for one in planner.json()["items"] if one["source"] == "syllabus"]
            # 다시 연결한 셈으로 두고 한 번 더 누르면 그 자리로 들어간다
            with respx.mock(assert_all_called=True) as router:
                route = router.post(GOOGLE_INSERT).mock(
                    return_value=httpx.Response(200, json={"id": "gcal-after"})
                )
                after = await http.post(path, json=sent)
                asked = json.loads(route.calls[0].request.content)["id"]
            again = await _events(world.user)

    assert (refused.status_code, refused.json()["error"]["code"]) == (409, "CALENDAR_NOT_CONNECTED")
    assert len(tasks) == 1  # 확정과 할 일은 남는다
    assert len(rows) == 1 and rows[0]["external_event_id"] is None  # 자리만 잡힌 상태
    assert after.json()["calendar_events_created"] == 1
    assert len(again) == 1 and again[0]["external_event_id"] == asked  # 일정은 하나다


async def test_a_fixed_time_moves_the_google_event_and_our_row(settings: Settings) -> None:
    """시각·제목을 고쳐 다시 확정하면 **우리 기록과 Google 일정**이 함께 움직인다."""
    tuned = _with_google(settings)
    async with committed() as world:
        await _connect_google(world.user, tuned.token_encryption_key)
        async with confirmed(tuned, world) as (client, syllabus_id, items):
            http = client(world.user)
            path = f"/api/v1/syllabi/{syllabus_id}/confirm"
            midterm = next(one for one in items if one["title"] == "중간고사")
            with respx.mock(assert_all_called=True) as router:
                insert = router.post(GOOGLE_INSERT).mock(
                    return_value=httpx.Response(200, json={"id": "gcal-midterm"})
                )
                await http.post(
                    path, json={"items": _chosen(items, "중간고사"), "register_calendar": True}
                )
                event_id = json.loads(insert.calls[0].request.content)["id"]
            with respx.mock(assert_all_called=True) as router:
                patch = router.patch(f"{GOOGLE_INSERT}/{event_id}").mock(
                    return_value=httpx.Response(200, json={"id": event_id})
                )
                moved = await http.post(
                    path,
                    json={
                        "items": [
                            {
                                "id": midterm["id"],
                                "title": "중간고사(온라인)",
                                "starts_at": "2026-10-22T13:00:00+09:00",
                                "selected": True,
                            }
                        ],
                        "register_calendar": True,
                    },
                )
                sent = json.loads(patch.calls[0].request.content)
            rows = await _events(world.user)

    assert moved.status_code == 200, moved.text
    assert moved.json() == _counts(1, 0, 0, moved=1)
    assert sent["summary"] == "[시험] 데이터베이스 · 중간고사(온라인)"  # 과목은 LMS 이름이다
    assert sent["start"]["dateTime"].startswith("2026-10-22T13:00")
    assert len(rows) == 1
    assert rows[0]["title"] == "[시험] 데이터베이스 · 중간고사(온라인)"
    assert rows[0]["starts_at"].astimezone(KST).isoformat() == "2026-10-22T13:00:00+09:00"
    assert rows[0]["external_event_id"] == event_id  # 같은 일정을 고쳤다
```

#### `api/tests/test_calendar_api.py`

```python
"""마감일 캘린더 등록 API(F-32). Google 호출은 respx로 가로챈다. SQL은 test_calendar_db.py가 본다."""

import json
from collections.abc import Iterator
from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta
from typing import Any

import httpx
import pytest
import respx
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from app.clock import current_time
from app.config import Settings, get_settings
from app.crypto import decrypt, encrypt
from app.eligibility import KST
from app.main import create_app
from app.repositories.calendar import (
    AssignmentDeadline,
    GoogleTokens,
    OpportunityDeadline,
    SyllabusDeadline,
    get_calendar_repo,
)
from app.schemas.calendar import CalendarEventView
from tests.conftest import USER_ID, make_token

EVENTS = "/api/v1/calendar/events"
API_BASE = "https://calendar.test/v3"
TOKEN_URL = "https://oauth.test/token"
INSERT = f"{API_BASE}/calendars/primary/events"
SYLLABUS_ITEM_ID = "00000000-0000-4000-8000-0000000000e5"
ASSIGNMENT_ID = "55555555-5555-4555-8555-555555555555"
OPPORTUNITY_ID = "00000000-0000-4000-8000-0000000000e1"
EVENT_ID = "00000000-0000-4000-8000-0000000000e2"
GCAL_ID = EVENT_ID.replace("-", "")  # Google 일정 번호는 기록 행의 id에서 만든다
NOW = datetime(2026, 10, 15, 3, 0, tzinfo=UTC)
DEADLINE = datetime(2026, 10, 24, 9, 0, tzinfo=UTC)  # 2026-10-24 18:00 KST


class FakeCalendarRepository:
    def __init__(self, key: str) -> None:
        self.key = key
        self.tokens_row: GoogleTokens | None = GoogleTokens(
            encrypt("access-1", key), encrypt("refresh-1", key), NOW + timedelta(minutes=30)
        )
        self.opportunity: OpportunityDeadline | None = OpportunityDeadline(
            OPPORTUNITY_ID, "한양브레인 장학금", DEADLINE
        )
        self.assignment: AssignmentDeadline | None = AssignmentDeadline(
            ASSIGNMENT_ID, "실습 2주차 결과 제출", "데이터베이스", DEADLINE
        )
        self.syllabus: SyllabusDeadline | None = SyllabusDeadline(
            SYLLABUS_ITEM_ID, "중간고사", "데이터베이스", "midterm", DEADLINE
        )
        self.events: dict[str, CalendarEventView] = {}
        self.saved: list[tuple[str, int]] = []
        self.added: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        self.attached: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
        self.removed: list[str] = []
        self.uncommitted: list[str] = []  # 아직 확정하지 않은 자리(rollback이 지운다)

    async def tokens(self, user_id: str) -> GoogleTokens | None:
        return self.tokens_row if user_id == USER_ID else None

    async def save_access_token(self, user_id: str, encrypted: str, expires_in: int) -> None:
        self.saved.append((decrypt(encrypted, self.key), expires_in))

    async def prepared_opportunity(
        self, user_id: str, opportunity_id: str
    ) -> OpportunityDeadline | None:
        if user_id != USER_ID or opportunity_id != OPPORTUNITY_ID:
            return None
        return self.opportunity

    async def assignment_deadline(
        self, user_id: str, assignment_id: str
    ) -> AssignmentDeadline | None:
        if user_id != USER_ID or assignment_id != ASSIGNMENT_ID:
            return None
        return self.assignment

    async def syllabus_deadline(self, user_id: str, item_id: str) -> SyllabusDeadline | None:
        if user_id != USER_ID or item_id != SYLLABUS_ITEM_ID:
            return None
        return self.syllabus

    async def event_for(
        self,
        user_id: str,
        provider: str,
        *,
        opportunity_id: str | None = None,
        lms_assignment_id: str | None = None,
        syllabus_item_id: str | None = None,
    ) -> CalendarEventView | None:
        target = opportunity_id or lms_assignment_id or syllabus_item_id
        return self.events.get(f"{target}:{provider}")

    async def one(self, user_id: str, event_id: str) -> CalendarEventView | None:
        if user_id != USER_ID:
            return None
        return next((e for e in self.events.values() if e.id == event_id), None)

    async def reserve(self, user_id: str, **values: Any) -> CalendarEventView:
        """실제 저장소처럼 `on conflict ... where external_event_id is null`을 흉내 낸다."""
        target = (
            values.get("opportunity_id")
            or values.get("lms_assignment_id")
            or values.get("syllabus_item_id")
        )
        key = f"{target}:{values['provider']}"
        found = self.events.get(key)
        if found is not None:  # 그새 다른 요청이 잡았거나, 넣다 만 자리가 남아 있다
            if found.external_event_id is not None:
                return found
            fresh = found.model_copy(
                update={"title": values["title"], "starts_at": values["starts_at"]}
            )
            self.events[key] = fresh
            return fresh
        self.added.append(values)
        event = CalendarEventView(
            id=EVENT_ID,
            provider=values["provider"],
            external_event_id=None,  # Google에 넣기 전이다
            title=values["title"],
            starts_at=values["starts_at"],
            all_day=False,
        )
        self.events[key] = event
        self.uncommitted.append(key)
        return event

    async def commit_reservation(self) -> None:
        self.uncommitted.clear()

    def rollback(self) -> None:
        """요청이 예외로 끝나면 커넥션이 하는 일(확정하지 않은 자리는 사라진다)."""
        for key in self.uncommitted:
            self.events.pop(key, None)
        self.uncommitted.clear()

    async def attach(self, user_id: str, event_id: str, external_event_id: str) -> None:
        self.attached.append((event_id, external_event_id))
        for key, event in self.events.items():
            if event.id == event_id:
                self.events[key] = event.model_copy(update={"external_event_id": external_event_id})

    async def remove(self, user_id: str, event_id: str) -> None:
        self.removed.append(event_id)
        for key, event in list(self.events.items()):
            if event.id == event_id:
                del self.events[key]


@pytest.fixture
def calendar(settings: Settings) -> FakeCalendarRepository:
    return FakeCalendarRepository(settings.token_encryption_key)


@pytest.fixture
def client(settings: Settings, calendar: FakeCalendarRepository) -> Iterator[TestClient]:
    tuned = settings.model_copy(
        update={
            "google_calendar_api_base": API_BASE,
            "google_token_url": TOKEN_URL,
            "google_oauth_client_id": "client-1",
            "google_oauth_client_secret": "secret-1",
        }
    )
    app = create_app(tuned)
    app.dependency_overrides[get_settings] = lambda: tuned
    app.dependency_overrides[get_calendar_repo] = lambda: calendar
    app.dependency_overrides[current_time] = lambda: NOW
    with TestClient(app) as test_client:
        yield test_client


@pytest.fixture
def auth() -> dict[str, str]:
    return {"Authorization": f"Bearer {make_token()}"}


def test_calendar_requires_login(client: TestClient) -> None:
    assert client.post(EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID}).status_code == 401
    assert client.delete(f"{EVENTS}/{EVENT_ID}").status_code == 401


def test_add_event_puts_an_hour_before_the_deadline(
    client: TestClient, auth: dict[str, str], calendar: FakeCalendarRepository
) -> None:
    """마감 시각으로 끝나는 1시간짜리를 KST로 넣는다."""
    with respx.mock(assert_all_called=True) as router:
        route = router.post(INSERT).mock(return_value=httpx.Response(200, json={"id": GCAL_ID}))
        res = client.post(EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID}, headers=auth)
    assert res.status_code == 201
    body = res.json()
    assert body["title"] == "[마감] 한양브레인 장학금"
    assert body["starts_at"] == "2026-10-24T17:00:00+09:00"
    assert (body["external_event_id"], body["all_day"]) == (GCAL_ID, False)
    assert calendar.attached == [(EVENT_ID, GCAL_ID)]
    sent = route.calls[0].request
    assert sent.headers["authorization"] == "Bearer access-1"
    payload = __import__("json").loads(sent.content)
    assert payload["id"] == GCAL_ID  # 일정 번호는 우리가 정한다(다시 넣어도 하나)
    assert payload["summary"] == "[마감] 한양브레인 장학금"
    assert payload["start"] == {"dateTime": "2026-10-24T17:00:00+09:00", "timeZone": "Asia/Seoul"}
    assert payload["end"] == {"dateTime": "2026-10-24T18:00:00+09:00", "timeZone": "Asia/Seoul"}


def test_adding_twice_keeps_one_event(
    client: TestClient, auth: dict[str, str], calendar: FakeCalendarRepository
) -> None:
    """다시 눌러도 Google을 또 부르지 않고 200과 그 일정이다(F-32 중복 등록 없음)."""
    with respx.mock(assert_all_called=True) as router:
        router.post(INSERT).mock(return_value=httpx.Response(200, json={"id": GCAL_ID}))
        first = client.post(EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID}, headers=auth)
    with respx.mock(assert_all_called=False) as router:
        route = router.post(INSERT).mock(return_value=httpx.Response(200, json={"id": "other"}))
        again = client.post(EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID}, headers=auth)
    assert (first.status_code, again.status_code) == (201, 200)
    assert again.json()["external_event_id"] == GCAL_ID and not route.called
    assert len(calendar.added) == 1


def test_an_expiring_token_is_refreshed_first(
    client: TestClient, auth: dict[str, str], calendar: FakeCalendarRepository
) -> None:
    """곧 만료될 토큰은 먼저 갱신해 저장하고, 새 토큰으로 넣는다."""
    calendar.tokens_row = GoogleTokens(
        encrypt("old", calendar.key),
        encrypt("refresh-1", calendar.key),
        NOW + timedelta(seconds=30),
    )
    with respx.mock(assert_all_called=True) as router:
        token_route = router.post(TOKEN_URL).mock(
            return_value=httpx.Response(200, json={"access_token": "access-2", "expires_in": 3599})
        )
        insert = router.post(INSERT).mock(return_value=httpx.Response(200, json={"id": GCAL_ID}))
        res = client.post(EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID}, headers=auth)
    assert res.status_code == 201
    assert insert.calls[0].request.headers["authorization"] == "Bearer access-2"
    assert calendar.saved == [("access-2", 3599)]
    sent = dict(httpx.QueryParams(token_route.calls[0].request.content.decode()))
    assert sent["grant_type"] == "refresh_token" and sent["refresh_token"] == "refresh-1"
    assert sent["client_id"] == "client-1"


def test_no_connection_asks_to_connect(
    client: TestClient, auth: dict[str, str], calendar: FakeCalendarRepository
) -> None:
    calendar.tokens_row = None
    res = client.post(EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID}, headers=auth)
    assert (res.status_code, res.json()["error"]["code"]) == (409, "CALENDAR_NOT_CONNECTED")
    assert res.json()["error"]["details"] == {"reason": "not_connected"}
    assert res.json()["error"]["message"] == "Google 캘린더를 연결해 주세요."


def test_a_refused_refresh_asks_to_connect_again(
    client: TestClient, auth: dict[str, str], calendar: FakeCalendarRepository
) -> None:
    """Google이 갱신을 거절하면(권한 취소 등) 다시 연결하게 한다."""
    calendar.tokens_row = GoogleTokens(
        encrypt("old", calendar.key), encrypt("refresh-1", calendar.key), NOW - timedelta(minutes=1)
    )
    with respx.mock(assert_all_called=True) as router:
        router.post(TOKEN_URL).mock(return_value=httpx.Response(400, json={"error": "invalid"}))
        res = client.post(EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID}, headers=auth)
    assert (res.status_code, res.json()["error"]["code"]) == (409, "CALENDAR_NOT_CONNECTED")
    assert res.json()["error"]["details"] == {"reason": "expired"}


def test_google_errors_become_502(
    client: TestClient, auth: dict[str, str], calendar: FakeCalendarRepository
) -> None:
    with respx.mock(assert_all_called=True) as router:
        router.post(INSERT).mock(return_value=httpx.Response(500, json={"error": "boom"}))
        res = client.post(EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID}, headers=auth)
    assert (res.status_code, res.json()["error"]["code"]) == (502, "GOOGLE_API_ERROR")
    assert "boom" not in res.json()["error"]["message"]  # 바깥 문구를 그대로 내보내지 않는다
    # 자리는 잡아 두되 번호는 붙지 않는다. 다시 누르면 같은 자리를 써서 캘린더에 하나만 들어간다
    assert calendar.attached == []
    calendar.rollback()  # 예외로 끝난 요청의 커넥션이 하는 일. 확정했으면 자리는 남는다
    assert calendar.events[f"{OPPORTUNITY_ID}:google"].external_event_id is None


def test_a_failed_google_call_keeps_the_place_so_the_retry_adds_one_event(
    client: TestClient, auth: dict[str, str], calendar: FakeCalendarRepository
) -> None:
    """Google이 502일 때 자리가 사라지면, 다시 눌렀을 때 **다른 번호**로 들어가 일정이 두 개가 된다.

    요청 커넥션은 예외가 나면 통째로 롤백되므로, 자리 잡기는 Google을 부르기 전에 확정해야 한다.
    """
    with respx.mock(assert_all_called=True) as router:
        router.post(INSERT).mock(return_value=httpx.Response(500, json={"error": "boom"}))
        client.post(EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID}, headers=auth)
    calendar.rollback()

    with respx.mock(assert_all_called=True) as router:
        insert = router.post(INSERT).mock(return_value=httpx.Response(200, json={"id": "gcal-1"}))
        again = client.post(EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID}, headers=auth)
    assert again.status_code == 201
    assert len(calendar.added) == 1  # 자리를 새로 만들지 않았다
    assert json.loads(insert.calls[0].request.content)["id"] == EVENT_ID.replace("-", "")


def test_a_place_left_over_from_a_changed_deadline_is_refreshed(
    client: TestClient, auth: dict[str, str], calendar: FakeCalendarRepository
) -> None:
    """넣다 만 자리가 남아 있고 그새 마감이 바뀌었으면, 그 자리의 제목·시각을 새로 맞춰 넣는다."""
    with respx.mock(assert_all_called=True) as router:
        router.post(INSERT).mock(return_value=httpx.Response(500, json={"error": "boom"}))
        client.post(EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID}, headers=auth)
    moved = DEADLINE + timedelta(days=1)
    calendar.opportunity = OpportunityDeadline(OPPORTUNITY_ID, "한양브레인 장학금 (연장)", moved)

    with respx.mock(assert_all_called=True) as router:
        insert = router.post(INSERT).mock(return_value=httpx.Response(200, json={"id": "gcal-1"}))
        body = client.post(EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID}, headers=auth).json()
    sent = json.loads(insert.calls[0].request.content)
    assert sent["end"]["dateTime"] == moved.astimezone(KST).isoformat()
    assert body["title"] == "[마감] 한양브레인 장학금 (연장)"
    assert body["starts_at"] == (moved - timedelta(hours=1)).astimezone(KST).isoformat()


def test_an_unprepared_or_undated_opportunity_is_refused(
    client: TestClient, auth: dict[str, str], calendar: FakeCalendarRepository
) -> None:
    calendar.opportunity = None
    res = client.post(EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID}, headers=auth)
    assert (res.status_code, res.json()["error"]["code"]) == (422, "VALIDATION_FAILED")
    assert res.json()["error"]["details"]["fields"] == {
        "opportunity_id": "준비 일정을 먼저 만들어 주세요."
    }
    calendar.opportunity = OpportunityDeadline(OPPORTUNITY_ID, "상시 공고", None)
    undated = client.post(EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID}, headers=auth)
    assert undated.status_code == 422
    assert undated.json()["error"]["details"]["fields"]["opportunity_id"].startswith(
        "마감일이 없는"
    )


def test_ics_is_not_ready_yet(client: TestClient, auth: dict[str, str]) -> None:
    res = client.post(
        EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID, "provider": "ics"}, headers=auth
    )
    assert res.status_code == 422
    assert res.json()["error"]["details"]["fields"]["provider"].startswith("지금은 Google")


def test_unknown_fields_are_refused(client: TestClient, auth: dict[str, str]) -> None:
    res = client.post(
        EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID, "syllabus_item_id": "x"}, headers=auth
    )
    assert res.status_code == 422


def test_delete_removes_it_from_google_too(
    client: TestClient, auth: dict[str, str], calendar: FakeCalendarRepository
) -> None:
    with respx.mock(assert_all_called=True) as router:
        router.post(INSERT).mock(return_value=httpx.Response(200, json={"id": GCAL_ID}))
        client.post(EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID}, headers=auth)
    with respx.mock(assert_all_called=True) as router:
        route = router.delete(f"{INSERT}/{GCAL_ID}").mock(return_value=httpx.Response(204))
        res = client.delete(f"{EVENTS}/{EVENT_ID}", headers=auth)
    assert res.status_code == 204 and route.called
    assert calendar.removed == [EVENT_ID] and calendar.events == {}


def test_deleting_an_event_google_already_lost_still_works(
    client: TestClient, auth: dict[str, str], calendar: FakeCalendarRepository
) -> None:
    """학생이 Google에서 먼저 지웠으면 404가 온다. 우리 기록도 지운다."""
    with respx.mock(assert_all_called=True) as router:
        router.post(INSERT).mock(return_value=httpx.Response(200, json={"id": GCAL_ID}))
        client.post(EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID}, headers=auth)
    with respx.mock(assert_all_called=True) as router:
        router.delete(f"{INSERT}/{GCAL_ID}").mock(return_value=httpx.Response(404))
        res = client.delete(f"{EVENTS}/{EVENT_ID}", headers=auth)
    assert res.status_code == 204 and calendar.removed == [EVENT_ID]


def test_someone_elses_event_is_not_found(client: TestClient, auth: dict[str, str]) -> None:
    res = client.delete(f"{EVENTS}/{EVENT_ID}", headers=auth)
    assert (res.status_code, res.json()["error"]["code"]) == (404, "NOT_FOUND")


def test_without_app_credentials_an_expired_token_asks_to_connect(
    settings: Settings, calendar: FakeCalendarRepository, auth: dict[str, str]
) -> None:
    """client_id·secret을 .env에 넣지 않으면 갱신할 수 없다. 다시 연결하게 한다(Google은 부르지 않는다)."""
    bare = settings.model_copy(
        update={"google_calendar_api_base": API_BASE, "google_token_url": TOKEN_URL}
    )
    app = create_app(bare)
    app.dependency_overrides[get_settings] = lambda: bare
    app.dependency_overrides[get_calendar_repo] = lambda: calendar
    app.dependency_overrides[current_time] = lambda: NOW
    calendar.tokens_row = GoogleTokens(
        encrypt("old", calendar.key), encrypt("refresh-1", calendar.key), NOW - timedelta(minutes=1)
    )
    with TestClient(app) as client, respx.mock(assert_all_called=False) as router:
        route = router.post(TOKEN_URL).mock(return_value=httpx.Response(200, json={}))
        res = client.post(EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID}, headers=auth)
    assert (res.status_code, res.json()["error"]["details"]) == (409, {"reason": "expired"})
    assert not route.called


def test_retrying_after_a_lost_response_keeps_one_event(
    client: TestClient, auth: dict[str, str], calendar: FakeCalendarRepository
) -> None:
    """넣는 응답을 못 받고 다시 눌러도 캘린더에 일정이 하나다.

    번호를 우리가 정해 보내므로 두 번째 요청은 같은 번호로 간다. Google은 409(같은 번호가 있다)를
    주는데 그것은 앞 요청이 이미 넣었다는 뜻이라 성공으로 본다. **그때 Google에 남은 시각은 앞
    요청이 보낸 것**이라, 이어서 한 번 고쳐 이번 마감으로 맞춘다.
    """
    with respx.mock(assert_all_called=True) as router:
        router.post(INSERT).mock(side_effect=httpx.ReadTimeout("늦었다"))
        lost = client.post(EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID}, headers=auth)
    assert lost.status_code == 502 and calendar.attached == []
    with respx.mock(assert_all_called=True) as router:
        route = router.post(INSERT).mock(
            return_value=httpx.Response(409, json={"error": "duplicate"})
        )
        fixed = router.patch(f"{API_BASE}/calendars/primary/events/{GCAL_ID}").mock(
            return_value=httpx.Response(200, json={"id": GCAL_ID})
        )
        again = client.post(EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID}, headers=auth)
    assert again.status_code == 201
    assert again.json()["external_event_id"] == GCAL_ID
    assert json.loads(route.calls[0].request.content)["id"] == GCAL_ID
    # 409 뒤에 시각을 맞춘다. 그 사이 마감이 바뀌었으면 캘린더가 옛 시각을 가리킨다
    sent = json.loads(fixed.calls[0].request.content)
    assert sent["end"]["dateTime"] == DEADLINE.astimezone(KST).isoformat()
    assert len(calendar.added) == 1  # 자리는 하나만 잡았다


def test_a_quota_error_is_not_a_permission_problem(
    client: TestClient, auth: dict[str, str]
) -> None:
    """403은 쿼터·한도에도 온다. 다시 연결하라고 하면 학생이 할 수 있는 일이 없다."""
    with respx.mock(assert_all_called=True) as router:
        router.post(INSERT).mock(
            return_value=httpx.Response(403, json={"error": "rateLimitExceeded"})
        )
        res = client.post(EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID}, headers=auth)
    assert (res.status_code, res.json()["error"]["code"]) == (502, "GOOGLE_API_ERROR")


def test_a_revoked_token_asks_to_connect_again(client: TestClient, auth: dict[str, str]) -> None:

    assert (res.status_code, res.json()["error"]["details"]) == (409, {"reason": "expired"})


def test_a_network_error_is_not_a_permission_problem(

) -> None:

    assert (res.status_code, res.json()["error"]["code"]) == (502, "GOOGLE_API_ERROR")


def test_a_passed_deadline_is_refused(
    client: TestClient, auth: dict[str, str], calendar: FakeCalendarRepository
) -> None:
    """마감이 지난 공고는 준비하기처럼 막는다(지난 일정을 캘린더에 넣지 않는다)."""
    calendar.opportunity = OpportunityDeadline(OPPORTUNITY_ID, "지난 장학", NOW - timedelta(days=1))
    res = client.post(EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID}, headers=auth)

def test_deleting_works_even_when_the_permission_is_gone(
    client: TestClient, auth: dict[str, str], calendar: FakeCalendarRepository
) -> None:
    """권한이 없어졌으면 Google에서는 못 지운다. 우리 기록은 지워 줄을 없앤다."""
    with respx.mock(assert_all_called=True) as router:
        router.post(INSERT).mock(return_value=httpx.Response(200, json={"id": GCAL_ID}))
        client.post(EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID}, headers=auth)
    calendar.tokens_row = None  # 설정에서 연결을 끊었다
    res = client.delete(f"{EVENTS}/{EVENT_ID}", headers=auth)
    assert res.status_code == 204
    assert calendar.removed == [EVENT_ID] and calendar.events == {}


def test_a_google_outage_keeps_the_record_for_a_retry(
    client: TestClient, auth: dict[str, str], calendar: FakeCalendarRepository
) -> None:
    """Google이 잠깐 멈춘 것은 다시 누르게 한다(기록을 지우면 Google 쪽 일정이 고아가 된다)."""
    with respx.mock(assert_all_called=True) as router:
        router.post(INSERT).mock(return_value=httpx.Response(200, json={"id": GCAL_ID}))
        client.post(EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID}, headers=auth)
    with respx.mock(assert_all_called=True) as router:
        router.delete(f"{INSERT}/{GCAL_ID}").mock(return_value=httpx.Response(500))
        res = client.delete(f"{EVENTS}/{EVENT_ID}", headers=auth)
    assert (res.status_code, res.json()["error"]["code"]) == (502, "GOOGLE_API_ERROR")

def test_a_broken_token_asks_to_connect_again(

) -> None:

    calendar.tokens_row = GoogleTokens("못-푸는-값", "못-푸는-값", NOW + timedelta(minutes=30))
    res = client.post(EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID}, headers=auth)
    assert (res.status_code, res.json()["error"]["details"]) == (409, {"reason": "expired"})


# ── LMS 과제 마감 (F-51) ────────────────────────────────────────────────────


def test_an_assignment_deadline_goes_in_with_the_course_in_the_title(
    client: TestClient, auth: dict[str, str], calendar: FakeCalendarRepository
) -> None:
    """제목에 과목을 앞세운다 — 캘린더에서 어느 수업 과제인지 바로 보인다."""
    with respx.mock(assert_all_called=True) as router:
        route = router.post(INSERT).mock(return_value=httpx.Response(200, json={"id": GCAL_ID}))
        response = client.post(EVENTS, json={"lms_assignment_id": ASSIGNMENT_ID}, headers=auth)
    assert response.status_code == 201
    body = response.json()
    assert body["title"] == "[과제] 데이터베이스 · 실습 2주차 결과 제출"
    assert body["external_event_id"] == GCAL_ID
    assert body["all_day"] is False
    sent = json.loads(route.calls[0].request.content)
    assert sent["summary"] == body["title"]
    # 마감 시각으로 끝나는 1시간이다(공고와 같은 규칙)
    assert sent["end"]["dateTime"].startswith(DEADLINE.astimezone(KST).isoformat()[:16])
    assert calendar.added[0]["lms_assignment_id"] == ASSIGNMENT_ID


def test_adding_the_same_assignment_twice_keeps_one_event(

    with respx.mock(assert_all_called=False) as router:

        again = client.post(EVENTS, json={"lms_assignment_id": ASSIGNMENT_ID}, headers=auth)
    assert (first.status_code, again.status_code) == (201, 200)
    assert not route.called

def test_exactly_one_target_is_required(client: TestClient, auth: dict[str, str]) -> None:
    both = {"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID, "lms_assignment_id": ASSIGNMENT_ID}
    assert client.post(EVENTS, json=both, headers=auth).status_code == 422
    assert client.post(EVENTS, json={}, headers=auth).status_code == 422
    assert client.post(EVENTS, json={"provider": "google"}, headers=auth).status_code == 422
    # 대상 셋 중 둘을 함께 보내도 422다(계획서 일정도 대상이 되었다)
    mixed = {"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID, "syllabus_item_id": SYLLABUS_ITEM_ID}
    assert client.post(EVENTS, json=mixed, headers=auth).status_code == 422
    three = {**both, "syllabus_item_id": SYLLABUS_ITEM_ID}
    assert client.post(EVENTS, json=three, headers=auth).status_code == 422


# ── 계획서 일정 (F-14) ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────


def test_a_syllabus_item_goes_in_starting_at_its_own_time(
    client: TestClient, auth: dict[str, str], calendar: FakeCalendarRepository
) -> None:
    """시험·발표는 **마감이 아니라 시작 시각**이다. 그 시각에 시작해 1시간 뒤에 끝낸다."""
    with respx.mock(assert_all_called=True) as router:
        route = router.post(INSERT).mock(return_value=httpx.Response(200, json={"id": GCAL_ID}))
        response = client.post(EVENTS, json={"syllabus_item_id": SYLLABUS_ITEM_ID}, headers=auth)
    assert response.status_code == 201
    body = response.json()
    assert body["title"] == "[시험] 데이터베이스 · 중간고사"
    assert body["starts_at"].startswith(DEADLINE.astimezone(KST).isoformat()[:16])
    sent = json.loads(route.calls[0].request.content)
    starts = DEADLINE.astimezone(KST)
    assert sent["start"]["dateTime"].startswith(starts.isoformat()[:16])
    assert sent["end"]["dateTime"].startswith((starts + timedelta(hours=1)).isoformat()[:16])
    assert calendar.added[0]["syllabus_item_id"] == SYLLABUS_ITEM_ID


def test_adding_the_same_syllabus_item_twice_keeps_one_event(

    with respx.mock(assert_all_called=False) as router:
        route = router.post(INSERT).mock(return_value=httpx.Response(200, json={"id": "other"}))
        again = client.post(EVENTS, json={"syllabus_item_id": SYLLABUS_ITEM_ID}, headers=auth)
    assert (first.status_code, again.status_code) == (201, 200)
    assert not route.called
    assert len(calendar.added) == 1

def test_an_unconfirmed_or_other_students_syllabus_item_is_not_found(

    """확인하지 않은 일정과 남의 계획서는 저장소가 돌려주지 않는다(404)."""
    calendar.syllabus = None

    other = "00000000-0000-4000-8000-0000000000ee"
```
